<sup>28</sup> And they joined themselves to Baal-peor, And ate the sacrifices of *things* dead.

<sup>29</sup> And they provoked *God* to anger by their works: And break in upon them did the plague.

<sup>30</sup> Then stood up Phinehas, and executed judgment: And stayed was the plague.

<!-- PAGE 987 -->

<sup>31</sup> And *that* was imputed to him for righteousness From generation to generation, even for ever.

<sup>32</sup> And they provoked *him* to anger at the waters of strife, And it turned out in to Moses on their account:

<sup>33</sup> For they embittered his spirit, And he spake [*rashly*] with his lips.

<sup>34</sup> They did not destroy the nations, As Jehovah had commanded them:

<sup>35</sup> And they were mingled with the heathen, And learned their works:

<sup>36</sup> And they served their idols; Which were to them for ruin.

<sup>37</sup> And they sacrificed their sons and their daughters to demons;

<sup>38</sup> And they shed innocent blood, The blood of their sons and of their daughters, Whom they sacrificed to the idols of Canaan; And polluted was the land with bloods.

<sup>39</sup> And they were defiled in their own works, And were adulterous in their own inventions.

<sup>40</sup> Then  <span style="color:#800000">ff122</span> burn did the wrath of Jehovah against his people, And he abhorred his inheritance;

<sup>41</sup> And he delivered them into the hands of the heathen; And those had dominion over them who hated them:

<sup>42</sup> And subdue them did their enemies, And they were afflicted under their hand.

<sup>43</sup> Many times did he deliver them; But they provoked *him* with their counsels, And were oppressed in their iniquity.

<sup>44</sup> Yet he saw when they were in distress, And heard their cry:

<sup>45</sup> And he remembered towards them his covenant; And it repented him according to the greatness of his mercies.

<!-- PAGE 988 -->

<sup>46</sup> And he made them to find pity Before all who had led them captive.

<sup>47</sup> Save us, O Jehovah, our God! And gather us from among the heathen, That we may celebrate thy holy name, That we may glory in thy praise.

<sup>48</sup> Blessed *be* Jehovah, the God of Israel, From everlasting to everlasting; And say let all the people, Amen. Praise ye Jehovah.

<p style="text-align:center" markdown="1">PART FIFTH.<sup>123</sup></p>

<p class="title-block-h2" style="text-align:center; font-size:16px; font-weight:bold; margin:18px 0 12px;" markdown="1"><span style="color:#006411">PSALM 107</span></p>

<sup>1</sup> “Celebrate Jehovah, for he is good, “For to everlasting is his mercy.”

<sup>2</sup> Say *this* let the redeemed of Jehovah, Whom he hath redeemed from the hand of the afflicter.

<sup>3</sup> Whom he hath gathered from the lands, From the east, and from the west, From the north, and from the south.

<sup>4</sup> They wandered from the way in the solitary desert; A city of habitation they found not:

<sup>5</sup> Hungry, also thirsty, *So that* their soul within them fainted.

<sup>6</sup> Then they cried to Jehovah in their distress, And from their troubles he delivered them.

<sup>7</sup> And he directed them by a right way, That they might come to a city of habitation.

<sup>8</sup> Let them celebrate before Jehovah his mercy, His marvelous works before the sons of men.

<sup>9</sup> For he hath satisfied the soul that longeth, And the soul that is famished he hath filled with good.

<!-- PAGE 989 -->

<sup>10</sup> Those who dwell in darkness, and in the shadow of death, Bound in affliction and iron;

<sup>11</sup> Because they rebelled against the words of God, And the counsel of the Most High they despised:

<sup>12</sup> And he humbled with trouble their heart; They were afflicted and *there was* none to help *them*.

<sup>13</sup> They cried to Jehovah in their distress: From their troubles he delivered them.

<sup>14</sup> He rescued them from darkness and the shadow of death, And their chains he broke asunder.

<sup>15</sup> Let them celebrate before Jehovah his mercy, And his marvelous works before the sons of men.

<sup>16</sup> For he hath broken the gates of brass, And the bars of iron he hath shivered.

<sup>17</sup> Fools on account of the way of their transgression; And on account of their iniquities are afflicted.

<sup>18</sup> All food loathe doth their soul; And they approach the gates of death.

<sup>19</sup> Then they cry to Jehovah in their trouble: From their straits he sayeth them.

<sup>20</sup> He sendeth his word, and healeth them, And rescueth them from all their corruptions.

<sup>21</sup> Let them celebrate before Jehovah his mercy, And his marvelous works before the sons of men.

<sup>22</sup> And let them sacrifice sacrifices of praise, And announce his works with exultation.

<sup>23</sup> Those who go down to the sea in ships, Trading in the great waters, <sup>24</sup> These men see the works of Jehovah, His wonders in the deep.

<!-- PAGE 990 -->

<sup>25</sup> He speaketh and raiseth the wind of storm, And lifteth high the waves thereof.

<sup>26</sup> They mount up to the heavens, They descend into the deeps; Their soul in  <span style="color:#800000">ff124</span> *their* distress is melted away.

<sup>27</sup> They are tossed and stagger as a drunken man, And all their skill is swallowed up.

<sup>28</sup> And they cry to Jehovah in their distress, And from their troubles he bringeth them out.

<p style="text-align:center" markdown="1"><sup>29</sup> He maketh the storm a calm, So that quiet are the waves thereof.</p>

<sup>30</sup> And they rejoice because they are stilled; And he leadeth them unto the haven of their desire.

<sup>31</sup> Let them celebrate before Jehovah his mercy, And his wonders before the sons of men;

<sup>32</sup> And let them exalt him in the congregation of the people, And in the assembly of the elders let them praise him.

<sup>33</sup> He turneth rivers into a desert, And springs of water into dryness;

<sup>34</sup> A land that is fruitful into saltness, For the wickedness of those who dwell therein.

<sup>35</sup> He turneth the desert into a pool of water, And the land of barrenness into springs of water.

<sup>36</sup> And there he causeth to dwell the famished, That they may prepare a city of habitation;

<sup>37</sup> And sow fields, and plant vineyards, And eat the fruit of increase.

<sup>38</sup> And he blesseth them, and they multiply exceedingly; And their cattle he doth not diminish,.

<sup>39</sup> Afterwards they are diminished and brought low, By reason of anguish, affliction, and sorrow.

<!-- PAGE 991 -->

<sup>40</sup> He poureth contempt upon princes, And maketh them to wander in the waste where there is no path.

<sup>41</sup> And he raiseth the afflicted from *his* misery, And maketh like a flock [*his*] families.

<sup>42</sup> See shall the upright ones and shall rejoice: And all iniquity shall stop her mouth.

<sup>43</sup> Who is wise, and he will observe these things, And they shall understand the loving-kindness of Jehovah?

<p class="title-block-h2" style="text-align:center; font-size:16px; font-weight:bold; margin:18px 0 12px;" markdown="1"><span style="color:#006411">PSALM 108</span></p>

<p style="text-align:center; color:#000080; margin:14px 2em;"><span style="color:#000080">*A Song of a Psalm of David*</span></p>

<sup>1</sup> Prepared *is* my heart, O God! prepared is my heart, I will chant and will sing psalms, even with my glory.

<sup>2</sup> Awake, psaltery and harp: I will arise at the dawn of day.

<sup>3</sup> I will celebrate thee, among the peoples, O Jehovah! And will sing psalms to thee among the nations:

<sup>4</sup> For great above the heavens, is thy goodness; And unto the clouds is thy truth.

<sup>5</sup> Be thou exalted above the heavens, O God! And above all the earth thy glory:

<sup>6</sup> That thy beloved ones may be delivered: Save by thy right hand, and hear me.

<sup>7</sup> God hath spoken in his holiness; I will rejoice: I will divide Shechem, And the valley of Succoth I will measure.

<sup>8</sup> Mine *is* Gilead, mine is Manasseh; And Ephraim *is* the strength of my head; Judah my lawgiver.

<sup>9</sup> Moab the pot for my washing; Over Edom will I cast my shoe; Over Palestina will I triumph.

<!-- PAGE 992 -->

<sup>10</sup> Who will lead me into the city fortified? Who will lead me even into Edom?

<sup>11</sup> Wilt not thou, O God! who hadst cast us off? And [*thou*] O God! [*who*] didst not go forth with our armies?

<sup>12</sup> Grant us relief from  <span style="color:#800000">ff125</span> trouble; For vain is the help of man.

<p style="text-align:center" markdown="1"><sup>13</sup> Through God we shall do valiantly, And he shall tread down our adversaries.</p>

<p class="title-block-h2" style="text-align:center; font-size:16px; font-weight:bold; margin:18px 0 12px;" markdown="1"><span style="color:#006411">PSALM 109</span></p>

<p style="text-align:center; color:#000080; margin:14px 2em;"><span style="color:#000080">*To the Chief Musician. A Psalm of David*</span></p>

<sup>1</sup> O God of my praise! be not silent;

<sup>2</sup> For the mouth of the ungodly man, and the mouth of deceit upon me are opened; They have talked with me with a tongue of guile:

<sup>3</sup> And with words of hatred they have encompassed me, And have fought against me without a cause.

<sup>4</sup> [*In return*] for my love they have been my adversaries; But I *set myself* to prayer.

<sup>5</sup> And they have requited me evil for good, And hatred for my love.

<sup>6</sup> Set thou over him an ungodly man; And let the adversary stand at his right hand.

<sup>7</sup> When he is judged let him go forth condemned,  <span style="color:#800000">ff126</span> And let his prayer be turned into sin.

<sup>8</sup> Let his days be few; His office let another receive.

<sup>9</sup> Let his children be fatherless, And his wife a widow.

<!-- PAGE 993 -->
<sup>10</sup> And in wandering let his children wander and beg,  <span style="color:#800000">ff127</span> And seek [*for bread*] out of their waste places. <span style="color:#800000">ff128</span> <sup>11</sup> Catch let the extortioner all which he hath, And let strangers plunder his labor.

<sup>12</sup> Let there be none prolonging mercy to him; And let there be none to have compassion upon his orphans.

<sup>13</sup> Let his posterity be destroyed;  <span style="color:#800000">ff129</span> In the generation following let their name be blotted out.

<sup>14</sup> Remembered let be the iniquity of his fathers before Jehovah; And the wickedness  <span style="color:#800000">ff130</span> of his mother let it not be blotted out.

<sup>15</sup> Let them be before Jehovah continually, And let him blot out from the earth their memorial;

<sup>16</sup> Because he remembered not to do mercy, And persecuted the man [*that was*] wretched and poor, And afflicted in heart, to put *him* to death.

<sup>17</sup> He loved cursing, and let it come upon him: He delighted not in blessing, and let it be far from him.

<sup>18</sup> And let him be clothed with cursing as with a garment; And let it come as water into his entrails, And as oil into his bones.

<sup>19</sup> Let it be to him as a mantle to cover him, And as a girdle with which continually he may gird himself <sup>20</sup> Let this be the recompense  <span style="color:#800000">ff131</span> of my adversaries from Jehovah, And of those who speak evil against my soul.

<sup>21</sup> But thou, O Jehovah, my Lord! deal thou with me for the sake of thy name: Because good *is* thy mercy, deliver me;

<sup>22</sup> For poor and needy I am, And my heart is wounded within me.

<p style="text-align:center" markdown="1"><sup>23</sup> As a shadow when it declineth, I walk about; I am shaken off as the locust.</p>

<sup>24</sup> My knees are become feeble through fasting; And my flesh hath fallen away from *its* fatness.

<!-- PAGE 994 -->

<sup>25</sup> But I am become a reproach to them; When they see me they shake their head.

<p style="text-align:center" markdown="1"><sup>26</sup> Help me, O Jehovah, my God! Save me according to thy mercy.</p>

<sup>27</sup> And they shall know that this is thy hand, *That* thou, O Jehovah! hast done it.

<sup>28</sup> They shall curse, but thou shalt bless; When they rise up they shall be put to shame; But thy servant shall rejoice.

<sup>29</sup> Clothed shall be my adversaries with disgrace, And they shall be covered as with a garment with their own shame.

<sup>30</sup> I will praise Jehovah greatly with my mouth; And in the midst of the great I will celebrate him:

<sup>31</sup> Because he standeth at the right hand of the poor one, To save from condemnations his soul.

<p class="title-block-h2" style="text-align:center; font-size:16px; font-weight:bold; margin:18px 0 12px;" markdown="1"><span style="color:#006411">PSALM 110</span></p>

<p style="text-align:center; color:#000080; margin:14px 2em;"><span style="color:#000080">*A Psalm of David*</span></p>

<sup>1</sup> Jehovah said to my Lord, “Sit thou at my right hand, “Until I make thy enemies thy footstool.”

<sup>2</sup> The scepter of thy power send shall Jehovah out of Zion: Rule thou in the midst of thy enemies.

<sup>3</sup> Thy people *shall come* with voluntary offerings, On the day *of the assembling* of thy army, In beauty of holiness: From the womb as from the morning dawn To thee *has been* the dew of thy youth.

<sup>4</sup> Sworn hath Jehovah, and he will not repent: “Thou art a priest for ever “According to the manner of Melchisedek.”

<p style="text-align:center" markdown="1"><sup>5</sup> The Lord at thy right hand Hath crushed in the day of his wrath kings.</p>

<!-- PAGE 995 -->

<sup>6</sup> He shall judge among the heathen, He shall fill *all* with ruins; He shall crush the head over a great country.

<sup>7</sup> Of the torrent in the way he shall drink, Therefore shall he lift up the head.

<p class="title-block-h2" style="text-align:center; font-size:16px; font-weight:bold; margin:18px 0 12px;" markdown="1"><span style="color:#006411">PSALM 111</span></p>

<p style="text-align:center; color:#000080; margin:14px 2em;"><span style="color:#000080">*Hallelujah*</span></p>

<sup>1</sup> <span style="color:#008080">a</span> I will celebrate Jehovah with *my* whole heart, <span style="color:#008080">b</span> In the congregation of the just, and in the assembly.

<sup>2</sup> <span style="color:#008080">g</span> Great are the works of Jehovah, <span style="color:#008080">d</span> Sought out of all who have pleasure in them.

<sup>3</sup> <span style="color:#008080">h</span> Beautiful and honorable  <span style="color:#800000">ff132</span> *is* his work; <span style="color:#008080">w</span> And his righteousness endureth for ever.

<sup>4</sup> <span style="color:#008080">z</span> He hath made a memorial by his wonderful works: <span style="color:#008080">j</span> Merciful and compassionate *is* Jehovah.

<sup>5</sup> <span style="color:#008080">f</span> A portion he hath given to those who fear him: <span style="color:#008080">y</span> He will remember for ever his covenant.

<sup>6</sup> <span style="color:#008080">k</span> The might of his works he hath proclaimed to his people, <span style="color:#008080">l</span> To give them the heritage of the heathen.

<p style="text-align:center" markdown="1"><sup>7</sup> <span style="color:#008080">m</span> The works of his hands *are* truth and judgment: <span style="color:#008080">n</span> True *are* all his statutes,</p>

<sup>8</sup> <span style="color:#008080">s</span> And established for ever — <span style="color:#008080">[</span> Made in truth and rectitude.

<sup>9</sup> <span style="color:#008080">p</span> Redemption he hath sent to his people; <span style="color:#008080">x</span> He hath commanded for ever his covenant: <span style="color:#008080">q</span> Holy and terrible *is* his name.

<sup>10</sup> <span style="color:#008080">r</span> The beginning of wisdom is the fear of Jehovah; <span style="color:#008080">ç</span> A good understanding *have* all those who do them: <span style="color:#008080">t</span> His praise endureth for ever.

<!-- PAGE 996 -->

<p class="title-block-h2" style="text-align:center; font-size:16px; font-weight:bold; margin:18px 0 12px;" markdown="1"><span style="color:#006411">PSALM 112</span></p>

<p style="text-align:center; color:#000080; margin:14px 2em;"><span style="color:#000080">*Hallelujah*</span></p>

<sup>1</sup> <span style="color:#008080">a</span> Blessed is the man who feareth Jehovah; <span style="color:#008080">b</span> In his commandments he will delight greatly.

<sup>2</sup> <span style="color:#008080">g</span> Mighty on the earth shall be his seed: <span style="color:#008080">d</span> The generation of the upright ones shall be blessed.

<sup>3</sup> <span style="color:#008080">h</span> Wealth and riches *shall be* in his house; <span style="color:#008080">w</span> And his righteousness endureth for ever.

<sup>4</sup> <span style="color:#008080">z</span> There ariseth in darkness light to the upright ones: <span style="color:#008080">j</span> *He is* merciful and compassionate and righteous.

<sup>5</sup> <span style="color:#008080">f</span> A good man showeth pity and lendeth; <span style="color:#008080">y</span> He manageth his affairs  <span style="color:#800000">ff133</span> in uprightness.

<sup>6</sup> <span style="color:#008080">k</span> Truly he shall not for ever be moved; <span style="color:#008080">l</span> In everlasting remembrance shall be the righteous one.

<sup>7</sup> <span style="color:#008080">m</span> Of evil tidings he shall not be afraid; <span style="color:#008080">n</span> Established is his heart, because he trusteth in Jehovah.

<sup>8</sup> <span style="color:#008080">s</span> Sustained *is* his heart, he shall not be afraid, <span style="color:#008080">[</span> Until he see *his desire* upon his adversaries.

<sup>9</sup> <span style="color:#008080">p</span> He hath dispersed; he hath given to the poor ones; <span style="color:#008080">x</span> His righteousness continueth for ever; <span style="color:#008080">q</span> His horn shall be exalted with glory.

<sup>10</sup> <span style="color:#008080">r</span> The ungodly one shall see *it*, and be incensed; <span style="color:#008080">ç</span> With his teeth he shall gnash and waste away: <span style="color:#008080">t</span> The desire of the ungodly ones shall perish.

<p class="title-block-h2" style="text-align:center; font-size:16px; font-weight:bold; margin:18px 0 12px;" markdown="1"><span style="color:#006411">PSALM 113</span></p>

<p style="text-align:center; color:#000080; margin:14px 2em;"><span style="color:#000080">*Hallelujah*</span></p>

<!-- PAGE 997 -->

<sup>1</sup> Praise ye, O ye servants of Jehovah! Praise ye the name of Jehovah.

<sup>2</sup> Let the name of Jehovah be blessed, From henceforth and for ever.

<sup>3</sup> From the rising of the sun even to his going down, Worthy to be praised *is* the name of Jehovah.

<sup>4</sup> High above all nations *is* Jehovah, Above the heavens is his glory.

<sup>5</sup> Who *is* like Jehovah our God, <sup>6</sup> Who hath his dwelling on high,  <span style="color:#800000">ff134</span> Who humbleth himself to behold *that which* is done in heaven and on earth?

<sup>7</sup> Who raiseth from the dust the needy one; From the dunghill he lifteth up the wretched one;

<sup>8</sup> To make him sit with princes, With the princes of his people.

<sup>9</sup> Who causeth the barren woman to dwell with a family, Rejoicing that *she is* a mother of children. Hallelujah.

<p class="title-block-h2" style="text-align:center; font-size:16px; font-weight:bold; margin:18px 0 12px;" markdown="1"><span style="color:#006411">PSALM 114</span></p>

<sup>1</sup> When Israel went forth from Egypt, The house of Jacob from a people barbarous;

<sup>2</sup> [*Then*] was Judah for his holiness, Israel, his dominions.

<sup>3</sup> The sea saw, and fled, Jordan was turned backward.

<sup>4</sup> The mountains leaped as rams, The hills as the lambs of the flock.

<sup>5</sup> What ailed thee, O sea! that thou fleddest? *And thou*, O Jordan! *that* thou wast turned back?

<!-- PAGE 998 -->

<sup>6</sup> Ye mountains, *that* ye leaped like rams; Ye hills, as the lambs of the flock? 7, At the presence of the Lord, tremble, O earth! At the presence of the God of Jacob;

<sup>8</sup> Who turned the rock into pools of water, The flinty rock into a fountain of waters.

<p class="title-block-h2" style="text-align:center; font-size:16px; font-weight:bold; margin:18px 0 12px;" markdown="1"><span style="color:#006411">PSALM 115</span></p>

<sup>1</sup> Not to us, O Jehovah! not to us, But to thy name give glory, On account of thy mercy, on account of thy truth.

<sup>2</sup> Why should the heathen say, “Where *is* now their God?”

<sup>3</sup> But our God *is* in the heaven: Whatever pleased him he hath done.

<sup>4</sup> Their idols *are* silver and gold, The work of the hands of men.

<sup>5</sup> A mouth have they,  <span style="color:#800000">ff135</span> and they do not speak: Eyes have they, and they do not see:

<sup>6</sup> Ears have they, and they do not hear: Noses have they, and they do not smell:

<sup>7</sup> Hands have they, and they do not feel: Feet have they, and they do not walk: They do not speak through their throat.

<p style="text-align:center" markdown="1"><sup>8</sup> Like to them shall be those who make them, And every one who trusteth in them.</p>

<sup>9</sup> O Israel! trust thou in Jehovah: Their help and their shield is He:

<sup>10</sup> O house of Aaron! trust in Jehovah: Their help and their shield is he:

<sup>11</sup> Ye who fear Jehovah! trust in Jehovah: Their help and their shield *is* He.

<sup>12</sup> Jehovah hath remembered us; he will bless us; He will bless the house of Israel; He will bless the house of Aaron.

<!-- PAGE 999 -->

<sup>13</sup> He will bless those who fear Jehovah, The small together with the great.

<sup>14</sup> Jehovah will add to you, To you and to your children.

<sup>15</sup> Blessed are ye of Jehovah, Who made heaven and earth.

<sup>16</sup> The heavens, the heavens *are* Jehovah’s: But the earth he hath given to the sons of men.

<sup>17</sup> The dead shall not praise *thee*, O God! Nor any who go down into silence.

<sup>18</sup> But as for us we will bless God, From this time, and even for evermore. Hallelujah.

<p class="title-block-h2" style="text-align:center; font-size:16px; font-weight:bold; margin:18px 0 12px;" markdown="1"><span style="color:#006411">PSALM 116</span></p>

<sup>1</sup> I have loved, because heard hath Jehovah the voice of my prayer;

<sup>2</sup> Because he hath inclined his ear to me, When in my straits I called upon him. <span style="color:#800000">ff136</span> <sup>3</sup> They encompassed me, the snares of death, And the sorrows of the grave found me: Trouble and grief I found.

<sup>4</sup> And on the name of Jehovah I will call: “I beseech thee, O Jehovah! deliver my soul.”

<sup>5</sup> Merciful *is* Jehovah and righteous; And our God *is* compassionate.

<p style="text-align:center" markdown="1"><sup>6</sup> Guard doth Jehovah the simple: I was brought low and he saved me.</p>

<sup>7</sup> Return, O my soul! to thy rest; For Jehovah hath dealt kindly with thee. <span style="color:#800000">ff137</span> <sup>8</sup> Because thou hast rescued my soul from death My eye from tears, My foot from falling.

<!-- PAGE 1000 -->

<sup>9</sup> I will walk before Jehovah in the land of the living.

<sup>10</sup> I have believed, for I will speak: “I am afflicted greatly.”

<sup>11</sup> I said in my fear, “Every man is a liar.”

<sup>12</sup> What shall I render to Jehovah? All his benefits *are* upon me.

<p style="text-align:center" markdown="1"><sup>13</sup> The cup of salvation I will take, And upon the name of Jehovah I will call.</p>

<sup>14</sup> My vows to, Jehovah I will perform Now in the presence of all his people.

<sup>15</sup> Precious in the eyes of Jehovah is the death of his meek ones.

<sup>16</sup> Come! O Jehovah! for I, *am* thy servant; I *am* thy servant, the son of thy handmaid; Thou hast broken my fetters.

<sup>17</sup> To thee will I sacrifice the sacrifice of praise, And upon the name of Jehovah will I call.

<sup>18</sup> My vows to Jehovah will I perform Now in the presence of all his people, <sup>19</sup> In the courts of the house of Jehovah In the midst of thee, O Jerusalem! Hallelujah.

<p class="title-block-h2" style="text-align:center; font-size:16px; font-weight:bold; margin:18px 0 12px;" markdown="1"><span style="color:#006411">PSALM 117</span></p>

<sup>1</sup> Praise ye Jehovah, all ye nations! Celebrate him all ye peoples!

<sup>2</sup> For great towards us is his mercy, And the truth of Jehovah *continueth* for ever. Hallelujah.

<p class="title-block-h2" style="text-align:center; font-size:16px; font-weight:bold; margin:18px 0 12px;" markdown="1"><span style="color:#006411">PSALM 118</span></p>

<p style="text-align:center" markdown="1"><sup>1</sup> Celebrate ye Jehovah! for *he is* good: For to everlasting *endureth* his mercy.</p>

<!-- PAGE 1001 -->

<sup>2</sup> Let now Israel say: “For  <span style="color:#800000">ff138</span> to everlasting *endureth* his mercy.”

<sup>3</sup> Let now the house of Aaron say: “For to everlasting *endureth* his mercy.”

<sup>4</sup> Let now those who fear Jehovah say: “For to everlasting *endureth* his mercy.”

<sup>5</sup> In my distress I called upon God; God answered me *by setting me* at large.

<sup>6</sup> Jehovah *is* with me: I will not fear what man can do to me.

<sup>7</sup> Jehovah *is* with me among those who help me, And I shall see *my desire* upon my adversaries.

<p style="text-align:center" markdown="1"><sup>8</sup> It is better to hope in Jehovah Than to place confidence in man:</p>

<sup>9</sup> It is better to hope in Jehovah Than to place confidence in princes.

<sup>10</sup> All the nations encompassed me: *But* in the name of Jehovah I will surely cut them off.

<sup>11</sup> They encompassed me, yea they encompassed me, *But* in the name of Jehovah I will surely cut them off’.

<sup>12</sup> They encompassed me as bees: They were extinguished as a fire of thorns: In the name of Jehovah I will surely cut them off.

<sup>13</sup> Thou hast thrust and  <span style="color:#800000">ff139</span> thrust at me, that I might fall: But Jehovah helped me.

<sup>14</sup> My strength and *my* song is God, And he hath become to me salvation.

<sup>15</sup> The voice of exultation and salvation is in the tabernacles of the righteous: The right hand of Jehovah hath done valiantly.

<sup>16</sup> The right hand of Jehovah is exalted, The right hand of Jehovah hath done valiantly.

<sup>17</sup> I shall not die, yea, I shall live, And recount the works of God.

<!-- PAGE 1002 -->

<sup>18</sup> Chastised me sorely hath  <span style="color:#800000">ff140</span> God; But he hath not delivered me up unto death.

<sup>19</sup> Open to me the gates of righteousness; *And* having entered into them, I will celebrate God.

<sup>20</sup> This is the gate of Jehovah, The just ones shall enter into it.

<sup>21</sup> I will celebrate thee because thou hast heard me, And hast been my salvation.

<sup>22</sup> The stone which the builders rejected Is become the head [*stone*] of the corner.

<sup>23</sup> By Jehovah hath this been done: It is marvelous in our eyes.

<sup>24</sup> This *is* the day which Jehovah hath made; Let us exult and rejoice in it.

<sup>25</sup> I beseech thee, O Jehovah! save me; I beseech thee, I beseech thee, O Jehovah! give prosperity I beseech thee.

<sup>26</sup> Blessed is he who cometh in the name of Jehovah: We bless you from the house of Jehovah.

<sup>27</sup> God *is* Jehovah, and he hath given light to us: Bind ye the lamb with cords, even to the horns of the altar.

<p style="text-align:center" markdown="1"><sup>28</sup> My God art thou, and I will celebrate thee: My God, I will exalt thee.</p>

<sup>29</sup> Celebrate ye Jehovah; for *he is* good: For to everlasting *is* his mercy.

<p class="title-block-h2" style="text-align:center; font-size:16px; font-weight:bold; margin:18px 0 12px;" markdown="1"><span style="color:#006411">PSALM 119</span></p>

<p style="text-align:center" markdown="1"><span style="color:#008080">a</span> [Aleph.]</p>

<sup>1</sup> <span style="color:#008080">a</span> Blessed *are* those who are upright in *their* way, Walking in the law of Jehovah.

<!-- PAGE 1003 -->

<sup>2</sup> <span style="color:#008080">a</span> Blessed *are* those who keep his testimonies, *Who* with *their* whole heart seek him.

<sup>3</sup> <span style="color:#008080">a</span>, Surely they do not work iniquity, *Who* in his ways do walk.

<sup>4</sup> <span style="color:#008080">a</span> Thou hast commanded That thy statutes be kept carefully.

<sup>5</sup> <span style="color:#008080">a</span> O that my ways may be directed To the keeping of thy statutes!

<sup>6</sup> <span style="color:#008080">a</span> Then shall I not be ashamed, When I shall have been attentive to all thy commandments.

<sup>7</sup> <span style="color:#008080">a</span> I will celebrate thee with uprightness of heart, When I shall have learned the judgments of thy righteousness.

<sup>8</sup> <span style="color:#008080">a</span>, Thy statutes I will keep: Do not thou forsake me very far.

<p style="text-align:center" markdown="1"><span style="color:#008080">b</span> [Beth.]</p>

<sup>9</sup> <span style="color:#008080">b</span> By what [*means*] shall a young man cleanse his way? By taking heed according to thy word.

<sup>10</sup> <span style="color:#008080">b</span> With my whole heart have I sought thee; Let me not go astray from thy commandments.

<sup>11</sup> <span style="color:#008080">b</span> In my heart I have hidden thy word, That I might not sin against thee.

<sup>12</sup> <span style="color:#008080">b</span> Blessed *art* thou, O Jehovah! Teach me thy statutes.

<p style="text-align:center" markdown="1"><sup>13</sup> <span style="color:#008080">b</span> With my lips have I declared All the judgments of thy mouth.</p>

<sup>14</sup> <span style="color:#008080">b</span> In the way of thy testimonies I have taken delight, As much as in all riches.

<sup>15</sup> <span style="color:#008080">b</span> Upon thy statutes will I meditate, And I will consider thy paths.

<sup>16</sup> <span style="color:#008080">b</span> In thy commandments will I delight myself; I will not forget thy words.

<!-- PAGE 1004 -->

<p style="text-align:center" markdown="1"><span style="color:#008080">g</span> [Gimel.]</p>

<sup>17</sup> <span style="color:#008080">g</span> Do good to thy servant, And I shall live and keep thy word.

<sup>18</sup> <span style="color:#008080">g</span> Open mine eyes, And I shall behold the wonders of thy law.

<sup>19</sup> <span style="color:#008080">g</span> I am a stranger on the earth: Hide not from me thy commandments.

<sup>20</sup> <span style="color:#008080">g</span> Broken is my soul with the desire *it hath* To thy judgments at all times.

<sup>21</sup> <span style="color:#008080">g</span> Thou hast destroyed the proud ones; Cursed are those who decline from thy commandments.

<sup>22</sup> <span style="color:#008080">g</span> Remove from me reproach and contempt; For thy testimonies I have kept.

<sup>23</sup> <span style="color:#008080">g</span> Princes also have sat, against me they have spoken: Thy servant meditated upon thy statutes.

<p style="text-align:center" markdown="1"><sup>24</sup> <span style="color:#008080">g</span> Also thy testimonies *are* my delights, The men of my counsel.</p>

<p style="text-align:center" markdown="1"><span style="color:#008080">d</span> [Daleth.]</p>

<p style="text-align:center" markdown="1"><sup>25</sup> <span style="color:#008080">d</span> Cleave to the dust doth my soul: Quicken me according to thy word.</p>

<sup>26</sup> <span style="color:#008080">d</span> I have declared my ways, and thou hast answered me: Teach me thy statutes.

<sup>27</sup> <span style="color:#008080">d</span> The way of thy commandments make me to understand, And I will meditate upon thy wonders.

<sup>28</sup> <span style="color:#008080">d</span> Drop away doth my soul for grief: Raise me up according to thy word.

<p style="text-align:center" markdown="1"><sup>29</sup> <span style="color:#008080">d</span> The way of falsehood remove from me, And thy law grant me graciously.</p>

<sup>30</sup> <span style="color:#008080">d</span> The way of truth I have chosen; Thy judgments I have set before me.

<!-- PAGE 1005 -->

<sup>31</sup> <span style="color:#008080">d</span> I have cleaved to thy testimonies, O Jehovah Let me not be put to shame.

<sup>32</sup> <span style="color:#008080">d</span> The way of thy statutes I will run, When thou shalt have enlarged my heart.

<p style="text-align:center" markdown="1"><span style="color:#008080">h</span> [He.]</p>

<sup>33</sup> <span style="color:#008080">h</span> Teach me, O Jehovah! the way of thy statutes, And I will keep it unto the end.

<sup>34</sup> <span style="color:#008080">h</span> Make me to understand, and I will observe thy law, And keep it with my whole heart.

<sup>35</sup> <span style="color:#008080">h</span> Direct me in the way of thy statutes; For in it is my delight.

<p style="text-align:center" markdown="1"><sup>36</sup> <span style="color:#008080">h</span> Incline my heart to thy testimonies, And not to covetousness.</p>

<sup>37</sup> <span style="color:#008080">h</span> Turn away my eyes from seeing vanity; In thy way quicken me.

<p style="text-align:center" markdown="1"><sup>38</sup> <span style="color:#008080">h</span> Confirm to thy servant thy word, Who to thy fear *is devoted*.</p>

<sup>39</sup> <span style="color:#008080">h</span> Remove my reproach which I have dreaded, For thy judgments are good.

<sup>40</sup> <span style="color:#008080">h</span> Behold! I have longed after thy commandments: In thy righteousness quicken me.

<p style="text-align:center" markdown="1"><span style="color:#008080">w</span>[Vau.]</p>

<sup>41</sup> <span style="color:#008080">w</span> And let come to me thy mercies, O Jehovah! Thy salvation according to thy word.

<sup>42</sup> <span style="color:#008080">w</span> And I will answer a word to him who reproacheth me; For I have trusted in thy word.

<sup>43</sup> <span style="color:#008080">w</span> And take not from my mouth the word of truth utterly: Because for thy judgments I wait.

<sup>44</sup> <span style="color:#008080">w</span> And I will keep thy law continually, For ever and ever.

<!-- PAGE 1006 -->

<sup>45</sup> <span style="color:#008080">w</span> And I will walk at large; For thy statutes I have sought.

<p style="text-align:center" markdown="1"><sup>46</sup> <span style="color:#008080">w</span> And I will speak of thy testimonies before kings, And will not be ashamed.</p>

<sup>47</sup> <span style="color:#008080">w</span> And I will delight myself in thy commandments Which I have loved.

<sup>48</sup> <span style="color:#008080">w</span> And I will lift up my hands to thy commandments Which I have loved; And I will meditate upon thy statutes.

<p style="text-align:center" markdown="1"><span style="color:#008080">z</span> [Zain.]</p>

<sup>49</sup> <span style="color:#008080">z</span> Remember thy word to thy servant, Upon which thou hast caused me to hope.

<sup>50</sup> <span style="color:#008080">z</span> This is my consolation in my affliction; For thy word revives me.

<sup>51</sup> <span style="color:#008080">z</span> The proud ones have derided me exceedingly: From thy law I have not declined.

<sup>52</sup> <span style="color:#008080">z</span> I remembered thy judgments of old, O Jehovah! And comforted myself.

<sup>53</sup> <span style="color:#008080">z</span> Terror hath seized upon me, Because of the ungodly who forsake thy law.

<sup>54</sup> <span style="color:#008080">z</span> Thy statutes have been songs to me In the house of my pilgrimage.

<sup>55</sup> <span style="color:#008080">z</span> I remembered in the night thy name, O Jehovah! And I will keep thy law.

<sup>56</sup> <span style="color:#008080">z</span> This is done to me, Because thy statutes I have observed.

<p style="text-align:center" markdown="1"><span style="color:#008080">j</span> [Cheth.]</p>

<!-- PAGE 1007 -->
<sup>57</sup> <span style="color:#008080">j</span> *Thou art* my portion, O Jehovah! I have said that I will keep thy words.  <span style="color:#800000">ff141</span> <sup>58</sup> <span style="color:#008080">j</span> I have sought thy face with my whole heart; Have mercy upon me according to thy word.

<sup>59</sup> <span style="color:#008080">j</span> I thought upon my ways, And turned my feet to thy testimonies.

<sup>60</sup> <span style="color:#008080">j</span> I made haste and delayed not To keep thy commandments.

<sup>61</sup> <span style="color:#008080">j</span> The cords of the ungodly have caught hold of me; Thy law I have not forgotten.

<sup>62</sup> <span style="color:#008080">j</span> At midnight I will rise to praise thee, Because of the judgments of thy righteousness.

<sup>63</sup> <span style="color:#008080">j</span> A companion am I to all who fear thee, And who keep thy statutes.

<p style="text-align:center" markdown="1"><sup>64</sup> <span style="color:#008080">j</span> Of thy mercy, O Jehovah! the earth is full: Thy statutes teach thou me.</p>

<p style="text-align:center" markdown="1"><span style="color:#008080">t</span> [Teth.]</p>

<sup>65</sup> <span style="color:#008080">t</span> Kindly hast thou dealt with thy servant, O Jehovah! According to thy word.

<sup>66</sup> <span style="color:#008080">t</span> Goodness of taste and knowledge teach me; For thy commandments I have believed.

<sup>67</sup> <span style="color:#008080">t</span> Before I was brought low I went astray; But now thy word I keep.

<sup>68</sup> <span style="color:#008080">t</span> Good *art* thou, and beneficent: Teach me thy statutes.

<sup>69</sup> <span style="color:#008080">t</span> Weaved a lie  <span style="color:#800000">ff142</span> against me have the proud ones; *But as for me* I with my whole heart will keep statutes.

<sup>70</sup> <span style="color:#008080">t</span> Fat as grease is their heart; *But as for me* I in thy law do delight.

<sup>71</sup> <span style="color:#008080">t</span> *It hath been* good for me that I have been afflicted, That I might learn thy statutes.

<!-- PAGE 1008 -->

<sup>72</sup> <span style="color:#008080">t</span> Better to me is the law of thy mouth Than thousands of gold and silver.

<p style="text-align:center" markdown="1"><span style="color:#008080">y</span> [Yod.]</p>

<sup>73</sup> <span style="color:#008080">y</span> Thy hands have made and formed me; Make me to understand, and I shall learn thy commandments.

<sup>74</sup> <span style="color:#008080">y</span> Those who fear thee shall see me and rejoice; Because for thy word I have waited.

<sup>75</sup> <span style="color:#008080">y</span> I have known, O Jehovah! that righteousness are thy judgments; And in truth thou hast humbled me.

<sup>76</sup> <span style="color:#008080">y</span> To thy word to thy servant.

<sup>77</sup> <span style="color:#008080">y</span> Let come to me thy compassions, and I shall live; For thy law is my delight.

<sup>78</sup> <span style="color:#008080">y</span> Ashamed let be the proud, for they have falsely wronged me: As for me I will meditate upon thy precepts.

<sup>79</sup> <span style="color:#008080">y</span> Let those turn unto me who fear thee, And have known thy testimonies.

<sup>80</sup> <span style="color:#008080">y</span> Let my heart be sound in thy statutes, That I may not be put to shame.

<p style="text-align:center" markdown="1"><span style="color:#008080">k</span> [Caph.]</p>

<sup>81</sup> <span style="color:#008080">k</span> Fainted for thy salvation hath my soul: For thy word I wait.

<sup>82</sup> <span style="color:#008080">k</span> Failed have my eyes [*in looking*] for thy word, While I say, “When wilt thou comfort me?”

<sup>83</sup> <span style="color:#008080">k</span> For I have been as a bottle in the smoke; *And yet* thy statutes I have not forgotten.

<sup>84</sup> <span style="color:#008080">k</span> How many are the days of thy servant? When wilt thou execute judgment on my persecutors?

<!-- PAGE 1009 -->

<sup>85</sup> <span style="color:#008080">k</span> The proud have digged pits for me, Which is not according to thy law.

<sup>86</sup> <span style="color:#008080">k</span> All thy commandments are truth: Deceitfully do they persecute me; *therefore* help thou me.

<sup>87</sup> <span style="color:#008080">k</span> Almost have they consumed me upon the earth; Yet I have not forsaken thy statutes.

<sup>88</sup> <span style="color:#008080">k</span> According to thy goodness quicken me; And I will keep the testimony of thy mouth.

<p style="text-align:center" markdown="1"><span style="color:#008080">l</span> [Lamed.]</p>

<sup>89</sup> <span style="color:#008080">l</span> For ever, O Jehovah! Thy word endureth in the heavens.

<sup>90</sup> <span style="color:#008080">l</span> From generation to generation *is* thy truth: Thou hast established the earth, and it abideth.

<sup>91</sup> <span style="color:#008080">l</span> By thy judgments they continue to this day; For all are thy servants.

<sup>92</sup> <span style="color:#008080">l</span> Unless thy law *had been* my delight, Then should I have perished in my affliction.

<sup>93</sup> <span style="color:#008080">l</span> Never will I forget thy statutes, For in them thou hast quickened me.

<sup>94</sup> <span style="color:#008080">l</span> Thine I am; O save me! For thy statutes I have sought.

<p style="text-align:center" markdown="1"><sup>95</sup> <span style="color:#008080">l</span> For me wait do the ungodly to destroy me: *But* I consider thy testimonies.</p>

<sup>96</sup> <span style="color:#008080">l</span> In all perfection I have seen an end: Broad is thy commandment exceedingly.

<p style="text-align:center" markdown="1"><span style="color:#008080">m</span> [Mem.]</p>

<sup>97</sup> <span style="color:#008080">m</span> O how have I loved thy law! All the day it is my meditation.

<sup>98</sup> <span style="color:#008080">m</span> Than my adversaries thou hast made me wiser by thy commandments; Because for ever they are with me.

<!-- PAGE 1010 -->

<sup>99</sup> <span style="color:#008080">m</span> Than all my teachers thou hast made me wiser; Because thy testimonies are my meditation.

<sup>100</sup> <span style="color:#008080">m</span> Than the aged I have more understanding; Because thy statutes I have observed.

<sup>101</sup> <span style="color:#008080">m</span> From every evil path I have restrained my feet, That I may keep thy word.

<sup>102</sup> <span style="color:#008080">m</span> From thy judgments I have not declined; For thou thyself hast taught me.

<sup>103</sup> <span style="color:#008080">m</span> How sweet to my palate have been thy words! More [*sweet*] than honey to my mouth!

<sup>104</sup> <span style="color:#008080">m</span> By thy statutes I have gotten understanding; Therefore I have hated every path of falsehood.

<p style="text-align:center" markdown="1"><span style="color:#008080">n</span> [Nun.]</p>

<sup>105</sup> <span style="color:#008080">n</span> A lamp to my feet is thy word, And a light to my path.

<sup>106</sup> <span style="color:#008080">n</span> I have sworn and will make sure, To observe the judgments of thy righteousness.

<sup>107</sup> <span style="color:#008080">n</span> I am afflicted exceedingly, O Jehovah! Quicken me according to thy word.

<sup>108</sup> <span style="color:#008080">n</span> I Let the freewill *offerings* of my mouth, I pray thee, be pleasing to thee, O Jehovah! And thy judgments teach me.

<sup>109</sup> <span style="color:#008080">n</span> My soul *is* in my hand continually; And thy law I have not forgotten.

<sup>110</sup> <span style="color:#008080">n</span> Laid have the ungodly a snare for me: Yet from thy statutes I have not erred.

<sup>111</sup> <span style="color:#008080">n</span> I have as [*my*] inheritance thy testimonies for ever; For the joy of my heart are they.

<!-- PAGE 1011 -->

<sup>112</sup> <span style="color:#008080">n</span> I have inclined my heart to perform thy statutes For ever, and even to the end.

<p style="text-align:center" markdown="1"><span style="color:#008080">s</span> [Samech.]</p>

<sup>113</sup> <span style="color:#008080">s</span> *Crooked* thoughts I have hated; But thy law I have loved.

<sup>114</sup> <span style="color:#008080">s</span> My hiding-place and my shield art thou: In thy word I have trusted.

<sup>115</sup> <span style="color:#008080">s</span> Depart from me, ye wicked men! And I will keep the commandments of my God.

<sup>116</sup> <span style="color:#008080">s</span> Sustain me by thy word, and I shall live: And make me not ashamed of my expectation.

<sup>117</sup> <span style="color:#008080">s</span> Establish me, and I shall be safe, And I will consider thy statutes continually.

<sup>118</sup> <span style="color:#008080">s</span> Thou hast trodden down all who go astray from thy statutes; For falsehood is their deceit.

<sup>119</sup> <span style="color:#008080">s</span> *As* dross thou hast made to cease all the ungodly of the earth; Therefore I have loved thy testimonies.

<sup>120</sup> <span style="color:#008080">s</span> Tremble  <span style="color:#800000">ff143</span> for fear of thee did my flesh, And of thy judgments I was afraid.

<p style="text-align:center" markdown="1"><span style="color:#008080">[</span> [Ain.]</p>

<sup>121</sup> <span style="color:#008080">[</span> I have done judgment and righteousness: Give me not up to my oppressors.

<sup>122</sup> <span style="color:#008080">[</span> Become surety for thy servant for good, That the proud may not oppress me.

<sup>123</sup> <span style="color:#008080">[</span> My eyes have failed for thy salvation, And for the word of thy righteousness.

<sup>124</sup> <span style="color:#008080">[</span> Deal with thy servant according to thy goodness, And thy statutes teach thou me.

<!-- PAGE 1012 -->

<sup>125</sup> <span style="color:#008080">[</span> Thy servant am I, give me understanding, That I may learn thy testimonies.

<sup>126</sup> <span style="color:#008080">[</span> *It is* time [*for thee*] to work, O Jehovah! *For they* have destroyed thy law.

<sup>127</sup> <span style="color:#008080">[</span> Therefore I have loved thy statutes Mere than gold and fine gold.

<sup>128</sup> <span style="color:#008080">[</span> Therefore all *thy* commandments I have esteemed *to be* altogether right: Every way of falsehood I have hated:

<p style="text-align:center" markdown="1"><span style="color:#008080">p</span> [Phe.]</p>

<sup>129</sup> <span style="color:#008080">p</span> Marvelous things *are* thy testimonies; Therefore observed them hath my soul.

<sup>130</sup> <span style="color:#008080">p</span> The entrance of thy words is light, Giving understanding to the little ones.

<p style="text-align:center" markdown="1"><sup>131</sup> <span style="color:#008080">p</span> My mouth I opened and panted, Because thy commandments I loved.</p>

<sup>132</sup> <span style="color:#008080">p</span> Look upon me and have pity upon me, According to *thy* judgment towards those who love thy name.

<sup>133</sup> <span style="color:#008080">p</span> My steps direct thou according to thy word, And let not any iniquity have dominion in me.

<sup>134</sup> <span style="color:#008080">p</span> Redeem me from the oppression of men, And I will keep thy precepts.

<p style="text-align:center" markdown="1"><sup>135</sup> <span style="color:#008080">p</span> Thy face cause thou to shine upon thy servant, And teach me thy statutes.</p>

<sup>136</sup> <span style="color:#008080">p</span> Rivers of waters run from my eyes, Because they have not kept thy law.

<p style="text-align:center" markdown="1"><span style="color:#008080">x</span> [Tsadde.]</p>

<sup>137</sup> <span style="color:#008080">x</span> Righteous art thou, O Jehovah! And right *are* thy judgments.

<!-- PAGE 1013 -->

<sup>138</sup> <span style="color:#008080">x</span> Thou hast commanded righteousness *in* thy testimonies, And truth exceedingly.

<sup>139</sup> <span style="color:#008080">x</span> Consumed me hath my zeal, Because forgotten thy words have my adversaries.

<sup>140</sup> <span style="color:#008080">x</span> Refined *is* thy word exceedingly: And thy servant hath loved it.

<sup>141</sup> <span style="color:#008080">x</span> Small *am* I, and despised; [*Yet*] thy precepts I have not forgotten.

<sup>142</sup> <span style="color:#008080">x</span> Thy righteousness *is* righteousness for ever, And thy law is truth.

<sup>143</sup> <span style="color:#008080">x</span> Trouble and anguish have come upon me; *But* thy commandments *have been* my delights.

<sup>144</sup> <span style="color:#008080">x</span> The righteousness of thy testimonies *endureth* for ever; Make me to understand, and I shall live.

<p style="text-align:center" markdown="1"><span style="color:#008080">q</span> [Koph.]</p>

<sup>145</sup> <span style="color:#008080">q</span> I have cried with my whole heart; Answer me, O Jehovah! *And* thy statutes I will keep.

<sup>146</sup> <span style="color:#008080">q</span> I have called upon thee, save me, And I will keep thy testimonies.

<sup>147</sup> <span style="color:#008080">q</span> I have prevented the dawn of the morning, <span style="color:#800000">ff144</span> and have cried: To thy word I have looked up.

<p style="text-align:center" markdown="1"><sup>148</sup> <span style="color:#008080">q</span> Prevented have my eyes the watches *of the night*, To meditate upon thy word.</p>

<sup>149</sup> <span style="color:#008080">q</span> My voice hear thou, according to thy mercy, O Jehovah According to thy judgment quicken me.

<sup>150</sup> <span style="color:#008080">q</span> Drawn near have the pursuers of malice; From thy law they have departed.

<!-- PAGE 1014 -->

<sup>151</sup> <span style="color:#008080">q</span> Near art thou, O Jehovah! And all thy commandments *are* truth.

<sup>152</sup> <span style="color:#008080">q</span> From the beginning I have known from thy testimonies, That for ever thou hast founded them.

<p style="text-align:center" markdown="1"><span style="color:#008080">r</span> [Resh.]</p>

<sup>153</sup> <span style="color:#008080">r</span> Behold my affliction, and rescue me; For thy law I have not forgotten.

<sup>154</sup> <span style="color:#008080">r</span> Debate my cause, and redeem me; According to thy word quicken me.

<sup>155</sup> <span style="color:#008080">r</span> Far from the ungodly is safety; Because thy statutes they have not sought.

<sup>156</sup> <span style="color:#008080">r</span> Thy compassions *are* great, O Jehovah! According to thy judgments quicken me.

<sup>157</sup> <span style="color:#008080">r</span> Many *are* my persecutors and my oppressors: From thy testimonies I have not declined.

<sup>158</sup> <span style="color:#008080">r</span> I saw the perfidious, and chid them; Because thy word they have not kept.

<sup>159</sup> <span style="color:#008080">r</span> Behold how thy commandments I have loved: According to thy clemency quicken me.

<sup>160</sup> <span style="color:#008080">r</span> The beginning of thy word *is* truth; And to everlasting *is* all the judgment of thy righteousness.

<p style="text-align:center" markdown="1"><span style="color:#008080">ç</span> [Shin.]</p>

<sup>161</sup> <span style="color:#008080">ç</span> Princes have persecuted me without a cause Yet at thy word afraid hath been my heart.

<sup>162</sup> <span style="color:#008080">ç</span> Joyful am I over thy word, As he who hath found much spoil.

<sup>163</sup> <span style="color:#008080">ç</span> Deceit I *have* hated and abhorred; Thy law I *have* loved.

<!-- PAGE 1015 -->

<sup>164</sup> <span style="color:#008080">ç</span> Seven times a day have I praised thee, Because of the judgments of thy righteousness.

<sup>165</sup> <span style="color:#008080">ç</span> Much peace have those who love thy law, And they have no stumbling-block.

<sup>166</sup> <span style="color:#008080">ç</span> I have waited for thy salvation, O Jehovah! And thy commandments I have done.

<sup>167</sup> <span style="color:#008080">ç</span> Kept hath my soul thy testimonies, And I have loved them exceedingly.

<sup>168</sup> <span style="color:#008080">ç</span> I have kept thy commandments and thy testimonies; For all my ways *are* before thee.

<p style="text-align:center" markdown="1"><span style="color:#008080">t</span> [Thau.]</p>

<sup>169</sup> <span style="color:#008080">t</span> Come let my cry into thy presence, O Jehovah! According to thy word give me understanding.

<sup>170</sup> <span style="color:#008080">t</span> Come let my prayer into thy presence: According to thy word deliver me.

<sup>171</sup> <span style="color:#008080">t</span> Speak shall my lips praise, When thou shalt have taught me thy statutes.

<sup>172</sup> <span style="color:#008080">t</span> Speak shall my tongue of thy word; For all thy precepts are righteousness.

<sup>173</sup> <span style="color:#008080">t</span> Let thy hand be to succor me; For thy commandments I have chosen.

<sup>174</sup> <span style="color:#008080">t</span> I have longed for thy salvation, O Jehovah! And thy law *hath been* my delights.

<p style="text-align:center" markdown="1"><sup>175</sup> <span style="color:#008080">t</span> Live let my soul, and let it praise thee; And let thy judgments succor me.</p>

<sup>176</sup> <span style="color:#008080">t</span> I have wandered like a lost sheep; Seek thy servant, for thy statutes I have not forgotten.

<!-- PAGE 1016 -->

<p class="title-block-h2" style="text-align:center; font-size:16px; font-weight:bold; margin:18px 0 12px;" markdown="1"><span style="color:#006411">PSALM 120</span></p>

<p style="text-align:center; color:#000080; margin:14px 2em;"><span style="color:#000080">*A Song of Degrees*</span></p>

<sup>1</sup> To Jehovah in my distress I cried, And he answered me.

<sup>2</sup> O Jehovah! deliver my soul from the lip of falsehood, From the tongue of deceit.

<sup>3</sup> What give thee, and what avail thee Shall the tongue of deceit?

<sup>4</sup> The arrows of a strong *man* sharpened, With coals of junipers.

<sup>5</sup> Alas for me! that I have been a sojourner in Meseeh, *And* have dwelt among the tents of Kedar.

<sup>6</sup> Long dwelt hath my soul with him who hateth peace.

<sup>7</sup> I [*am for*] peace; But when I speak, they [*are*] for war.

<p class="title-block-h2" style="text-align:center; font-size:16px; font-weight:bold; margin:18px 0 12px;" markdown="1"><span style="color:#006411">PSALM 121</span></p>

<p style="text-align:center; color:#000080; margin:14px 2em;"><span style="color:#000080">*A Song of Degrees*</span></p>

<sup>1</sup> I will lift up my eyes to the mountains, Whence come shall my help.

<sup>2</sup> My help *cometh* from Jehovah, Who made heaven and earth.

<sup>3</sup> He will not suffer thy foot to stumble; Slumber not shall thy guardian.

<sup>4</sup> Behold! slumber not nor sleep Shall the guardian of Israel.

<sup>5</sup> Jehovah *is* thy guardian; Jehovah *is thy* defense on thy right hand.

<sup>6</sup> By day the sun shall not smite thee, Nor the moon by night.

<sup>7</sup> Jehovah shall keep thee from all evil; He shall keep thy soul.

<sup>8</sup> Jehovah shall keep thy going out and thy coming in, From this time, and for ever.

<!-- PAGE 1017 -->

<p class="title-block-h2" style="text-align:center; font-size:16px; font-weight:bold; margin:18px 0 12px;" markdown="1"><span style="color:#006411">PSALM 122</span></p>

<p style="text-align:center; color:#000080; margin:14px 2em;"><span style="color:#000080">*A Song of Degrees Of David*</span></p>

<p style="text-align:center" markdown="1"><sup>1</sup> I was glad when they said to me, “Into the house of Jehovah we will go.</p>

<sup>2</sup> “Our feet shall be standing in thy gates, O Jerusalem!”

<sup>3</sup> Jerusalem built as a city, compact in itself together.

<sup>4</sup> Thither ascended the tribes, the tribes of God, For a testimony to Israel, To celebrate the name of Jehovah.

<sup>5</sup> For there have sat  <span style="color:#800000">ff145</span> thrones for judgment, The thrones of the house of David.

<sup>6</sup> Pray ye for the peace of Jerusalem: Prosper may those who love thee!

<sup>7</sup> Peace be within thy bulwark! Prosperity within thy towers!

<sup>8</sup> For the sake of my brethren and my neighbors, I will say now, “Peace [*be*] within thee! “ <sup>9</sup> For the sake of the house of Jehovah our God, I will seek good for thee.

<p class="title-block-h2" style="text-align:center; font-size:16px; font-weight:bold; margin:18px 0 12px;" markdown="1"><span style="color:#006411">PSALM 123</span></p>

<p style="text-align:center; color:#000080; margin:14px 2em;"><span style="color:#000080">*A Song of Degrees*</span></p>

<sup>1</sup> To thee will I lift up mine eyes, [*O thou*] who dwellest in the heavens!

<sup>2</sup> Behold! as the eyes of servants look to the hand of their masters; As the eyes of a handmaid *look* to the hand of her mistress, So our eyes [*look*] to Jehovah our God, Until he have mercy upon us.

<sup>3</sup> Have mercy upon us, O Jehovah! have mercy upon us; For we are exceedingly filled with reproach.

<!-- PAGE 1018 -->

<sup>4</sup> Exceedingly filled in itself is our soul With the mockery of the rich, With the contempt of the proud.

<p class="title-block-h2" style="text-align:center; font-size:16px; font-weight:bold; margin:18px 0 12px;" markdown="1"><span style="color:#006411">PSALM 124</span></p>

<p style="text-align:center; color:#000080; margin:14px 2em;"><span style="color:#000080">*A Song of Degrees. Of David*</span></p>

<p style="text-align:center" markdown="1"><sup>1</sup> “But for Jehovah who was on our side” — Say now may Israel:</p>

<sup>2</sup> “But for Jehovah who was on our side, “When men rose up against us;

<sup>3</sup> “Then alive had they swallowed us up, “When kindled was their wrath against us;

<sup>4</sup> “Then the waters had overwhelmed us, “The torrent had gone over our soul:

<sup>5</sup> “Then had gone over our soul the proud waters.”

<sup>6</sup> Blessed *be* Jehovah, Who gave us not for a prey to their teeth.

<sup>7</sup> Our soul as a bird, hath been rescued  <span style="color:#800000">ff146</span> from the snare of the fowlers; The snare hath been broken, and we have been delivered.

<sup>8</sup> Our help *is* in the name of Jehovah, Who made heaven and earth.

<p class="title-block-h2" style="text-align:center; font-size:16px; font-weight:bold; margin:18px 0 12px;" markdown="1"><span style="color:#006411">PSALM 125</span></p>

<p style="text-align:center; color:#000080; margin:14px 2em;"><span style="color:#000080">*A Song of Degrees*</span></p>

<sup>1</sup> They who confide in Jehovah *are as* mount Zion, *Which* shall not be removed, *but* shall abide for ever.

<sup>2</sup> As the mountains *are* about Jerusalem, So Jehovah *is* round about his people, From henceforth, and for ever.

<!-- PAGE 1019 -->

<sup>3</sup> For rest not shall the scepter of the ungodly ones upon the lot of the just, Lest the just ones should put forth to iniquity their hands.

<p style="text-align:center" markdown="1"><sup>4</sup> Do good, O Jehovah! to the good, And to *those who* are upright in their hearts.</p>

<sup>5</sup> But those who turn aside into their crooked paths, Jehovah shall make them walk with the workers of iniquity: *But there shall be* peace upon Israel.

<p class="title-block-h2" style="text-align:center; font-size:16px; font-weight:bold; margin:18px 0 12px;" markdown="1"><span style="color:#006411">PSALM 126</span></p>

<p style="text-align:center; color:#000080; margin:14px 2em;"><span style="color:#000080">*A Song of Degrees*</span></p>

<sup>1</sup> When bring back did Jehovah the captivity of Zion, We were like those that dream.

<sup>2</sup> Now filled with laughter *shall* be our mouth, And our tongue with exultation: Now shall they say among the heathen, “Great things Jehovah hath done for them.”

<sup>3</sup> Great things Jehovah hath done for us, *Whereof* we have been made glad.

<sup>4</sup> Bring back, O Jehovah! our captivity, As rivers in the South.

<sup>5</sup> They who sow in tears In exultation shall reap.

<sup>6</sup> Going forth, he shall go and weep, Carrying the price of the seed: Returning he shall return with exultation, Bearing his sheaves.

<p class="title-block-h2" style="text-align:center; font-size:16px; font-weight:bold; margin:18px 0 12px;" markdown="1"><span style="color:#006411">PSALM 127</span></p>

<p style="text-align:center; color:#000080; margin:14px 2em;"><span style="color:#000080">*A Song of Degrees. Of Solomon*</span></p>

<sup>1</sup> Except Jehovah build the house, In vain they labor that build it; Except Jehovah keep the city, In vain watcheth the watchman.

<!-- PAGE 1020 -->

<sup>2</sup> Vain *is it* for you early to hasten to rise, To go late to rest, To eat the bread of sorrows: *For* thus will he give to his beloved one sleep.

<sup>3</sup> Lo! the heritage of Jehovah are children: The reward *which he bestows* is the fruit of the womb.

<sup>4</sup> As arrows in the hand of a strong man, So *are* the sons of youth.

<sup>5</sup> Blessed is the man who shall have filled his quiver with them; *For* they shall not be ashamed when they speak with their enemies in the gate.

<p class="title-block-h2" style="text-align:center; font-size:16px; font-weight:bold; margin:18px 0 12px;" markdown="1"><span style="color:#006411">PSALM 128</span></p>

<p style="text-align:center; color:#000080; margin:14px 2em;"><span style="color:#000080">*A Song of Degrees*</span></p>

<sup>1</sup> Blessed *is* the man who feareth Jehovah, Who walketh in his ways.

<sup>2</sup> The labor of thy hands when thou shalt eat thou shalt be blessed, And it *shall be* well with thee.

<sup>3</sup> Thy wife *shall be* as a vine fruitful on the sides of thy house: Thy children as plants of olives around thy table.

<sup>4</sup> Lo! surely, thus blessed shall be the man who feareth Jehovah.

<sup>5</sup> Bless thee shall Jehovah from Zion; And thou shalt see the good of Jerusalem all the days of thy life.

<sup>6</sup> And thou shalt see thy children’s children, *And* peace upon Israel.

<p class="title-block-h2" style="text-align:center; font-size:16px; font-weight:bold; margin:18px 0 12px;" markdown="1"><span style="color:#006411">PSALM 129</span></p>

<p style="text-align:center; color:#000080; margin:14px 2em;"><span style="color:#000080">*A Song of Degrees*</span></p>

<!-- PAGE 1021 -->

<sup>1</sup> “Often have they afflicted me from my youths” Say now let Israel:

<sup>2</sup> “Often have they afflicted me from my youth; “Yet they have not prevailed against me.

<p style="text-align:center" markdown="1"><sup>3</sup> “Upon my back ploughed have the ploughers; “They have made long their furrows.</p>

<sup>4</sup> “Jehovah, who is righteous, hath cut asunder the cords of the wicked.”

<sup>5</sup> Confounded and turned backward shall be all who hate Zion.

<sup>6</sup> They shall be as the grass of the housetops, Which before it cometh forth is withered:

<sup>7</sup> With which the mower hath not filled his hand, Nor his bosom he who gathereth handfuls.

<sup>8</sup> Nor have they said who pass by, “The blessing of Jehovah *be* upon you, “We bless you in the name of Jehovah.”

<p class="title-block-h2" style="text-align:center; font-size:16px; font-weight:bold; margin:18px 0 12px;" markdown="1"><span style="color:#006411">PSALM 130</span></p>

<p style="text-align:center; color:#000080; margin:14px 2em;"><span style="color:#000080">*A Song of Degrees*</span></p>

<sup>1</sup> Out of the depths have I cried to thee, O Jehovah!

<sup>2</sup> O Lord! hear my voice; Let thy ears be attentive to the voice of my prayers.

<sup>3</sup> If iniquities thou shouldest mark, O God! O Lord! who shall stand?

<sup>4</sup> For with thee is forgiveness that thou mayest be feared.

<sup>5</sup> I have waited for Jehovah, waited hath my soul; And in his word have I hoped.

<sup>6</sup> My soul [*hath waited*] for the Lord Before the watchers of the morning, the watchers of the morning.

<!-- PAGE 1022 -->

<sup>7</sup> But  <span style="color:#800000">ff147</span> hope let Israel in Jehovah; For with Jehovah *there is* mercy, And with him there is plenteous redemption.

<sup>8</sup> And he shall redeem Israel from all his iniquities.

<p class="title-block-h2" style="text-align:center; font-size:16px; font-weight:bold; margin:18px 0 12px;" markdown="1"><span style="color:#006411">PSALM 131</span></p>

<p style="text-align:center; color:#000080; margin:14px 2em;"><span style="color:#000080">*A Song of Degrees. Of David*</span></p>

<sup>1</sup> O Jehovah! my heart hath not been elated, Nor lifted up have been my eyes, Nor have I exercised myself in great matters, Or in things hidden from me.

<sup>2</sup> If I have not framed [*or set*] and quieted my soul, Like one that is weaned from his mother, —  <span style="color:#800000">ff148</span> Like a child that is weaned is my soul upon me.

<sup>3</sup> Hope shall Israel in Jehovah From henceforth, and for ever.

<p class="title-block-h2" style="text-align:center; font-size:16px; font-weight:bold; margin:18px 0 12px;" markdown="1"><span style="color:#006411">PSALM 132</span></p>

<p style="text-align:center; color:#000080; margin:14px 2em;"><span style="color:#000080">*A Song of Degrees*</span></p>

<sup>1</sup> Remember, O Jehovah! David With all his affliction:

<sup>2</sup> Who sware to Jehovah, [*Who*] vowed to the Mighty One of Jacob:

<sup>3</sup> “If I shall enter into the tabernacle of my house, “If I shall go up upon the covering of my bed, <sup>4</sup> “If I give sleep to my eyes, “To my eyelids slumber, <sup>5</sup> “Until I find a place for Jehovah, “Habitations for the mighty God of Jacob,” —  <span style="color:#800000">ff149</span> <sup>6</sup> Lo! we heard of it at Ephratha; We found it in the fields of the wood.

<sup>7</sup> We will enter into his habitations, We will worship at his footstool.

<!-- PAGE 1023 -->

<sup>8</sup> Arise, O Jehovah! into thy rest, Thou and the ark of thy strength.

<p style="text-align:center" markdown="1"><sup>9</sup> Let thy priests be clothed with righteousness, And let thy meek ones exult.</p>

<sup>10</sup> For the sake of David thy servant Turn not away the face of thy Christ.

<sup>11</sup> Sworn hath Jehovah to David in truth, Nor will he turn from it: “Of the fruit of thy belly will I set upon thy throne.

<sup>12</sup> “If thy children shall keep my covenant, “And my testimonies, which I will teach them; “Also their children for ever shall sit upon thy throne.”

<sup>13</sup> For chosen hath Jehovah Zion; He hath desired her for a habitation to himself.

<sup>14</sup> “This is my rest for ever; “Here will I dwell, because I have loved her.

<sup>15</sup> “Her provision blessing I will bless; “Her poor I will satisfy with bread; .

<sup>16</sup> “And her priests I will clothe with salvation; “And her merciful ones with exultation shall exult.

<sup>17</sup> “There will I make to bud the horn of David: “I have prepared a lamp for my Christ.

<sup>18</sup> “His enemies will I clothe with shame, “And upon him flourish shall his crown.”

<p class="title-block-h2" style="text-align:center; font-size:16px; font-weight:bold; margin:18px 0 12px;" markdown="1"><span style="color:#006411">PSALM 133</span></p>

<p style="text-align:center; color:#000080; margin:14px 2em;"><span style="color:#000080">*A Song of Degrees. Of David*</span></p>

<sup>1</sup> Behold! how good, and how becoming, That brethren should even dwell together!

<!-- PAGE 1024 -->

<sup>2</sup> Like the precious oil upon the head, Which descendeth  <span style="color:#800000">ff150</span> upon the beard, the beard of Aaron, Which descendeth upon the skirt of his garments.

<sup>3</sup> Like the dew of Hermon, Which descendeth upon the mountains of Zion: For there commanded hath Jehovah the blessing, Life for evermore.

<p class="title-block-h2" style="text-align:center; font-size:16px; font-weight:bold; margin:18px 0 12px;" markdown="1"><span style="color:#006411">PSALM 134</span></p>

<p style="text-align:center; color:#000080; margin:14px 2em;"><span style="color:#000080">*A Song of Degrees*</span></p>

<sup>1</sup> Behold! bless ye Jehovah, All ye servants of Jehovah! Who stand in the house of Jehovah nightly.

<sup>2</sup> Lift up your hands to the sanctuary, And bless ye Jehovah.

<sup>3</sup> May Jehovah bless thee out of Zion, [*Even he*] who hath made heaven and earth.

<p class="title-block-h2" style="text-align:center; font-size:16px; font-weight:bold; margin:18px 0 12px;" markdown="1"><span style="color:#006411">PSALM 135</span></p>

<p style="text-align:center; color:#000080; margin:14px 2em;"><span style="color:#000080">*Hallelujah*</span></p>

<sup>1</sup> Praise ye the name of Jehovah; Praise [*it*] O ye servants of Jehovah!

<sup>2</sup> Who stand in the house of Jehovah, [*And ye who stand*] in the courts of the house of our God.

<sup>3</sup> Praise God; for good *is* Jehovah: Sing to his name; for it is pleasant:

<sup>4</sup> For God hath chosen Jacob to himself, Israel for his own possession.

<sup>5</sup> For I know that great is Jehovah, And *that* our God is above all gods.

<!-- PAGE 1025 -->

<sup>6</sup> All things which Jehovah pleaseth, lie doeth in the heavens and on the earth, In the sea and in all the deeps.

<sup>7</sup> Causing the clouds to ascend from the end of the earth: The lightnings for the rain he maketh, Bringing forth the wind out of his secret places.

<sup>8</sup> He smote the first born of Egypt, From man even unto beast.

<sup>9</sup> He sent signs and wonders in the midst of thee, O Egypt! On Pharaoh and on all his servants.

<sup>10</sup> He smote great nations, And slew mighty kings.

<sup>11</sup> Sihon, king of the Amorites, And Og the king of Basham, And all the kingdoms of Canaan.

<sup>12</sup> And gave their land for an heritage, An heritage to Israel, his people.

<sup>13</sup> O Jehovah! thy name *is* for ever; O Jehovah! thy memorial *is* from generation to generation.

<sup>14</sup> For judge will Jehovah his people, And concerning his servants he will repent himself.

<sup>15</sup> The images of the nations *are* silver and gold, The work of the hands of man.

<sup>16</sup> A mouth have they, and they will not speak;  <span style="color:#800000">ff151</span> Eyes have they, and they will not see.

<sup>17</sup> Ears have they, and they will not hear: Also, there is no breath in their mouth.

<sup>18</sup> Like unto them are those who make them, *And* every one who trusteth in them.

<p style="text-align:center" markdown="1"><sup>19</sup> O house of Israel! bless ye Jehovah; O house of Aaron! bless ye Jehovah;</p>

<sup>20</sup> O house of Levi! bless ye Jehovah; O ye who fear Jehovah! bless ye Jehovah.

<!-- PAGE 1026 -->

<sup>21</sup> Blessed *be* Jehovah out of Zion, Who dwelleth at Jerusalem. Hallelujah.

<p class="title-block-h2" style="text-align:center; font-size:16px; font-weight:bold; margin:18px 0 12px;" markdown="1"><span style="color:#006411">PSALM 136</span></p>

<sup>1</sup> Praise Jehovah, for *he is* good,  For to everlasting *endureth* his mercy.

<sup>2</sup> Praise the God of gods, For to everlasting *endureth* his mercy.

<sup>3</sup> Praise the Lord of lords, For to everlasting *endureth* his mercy.

<p style="text-align:center" markdown="1"><sup>4</sup> Who hath done wonders great alone, For to everlasting *endureth* his mercy.</p>

<sup>5</sup> Who hath made the heavens in wisdom, For to everlasting *endureth* his mercy.

<sup>6</sup> Who hath stretched out the earth above the waters, For to everlasting *endureth* his mercy.

<sup>7</sup> Who hath made the great lights, For to everlasting *endureth* his mercy.

<sup>8</sup> The sun for rule by day, For to everlasting *endureth* his mercy.

<sup>9</sup> The moon and stars for rule by night, For to everlasting *endureth* his mercy.

<sup>10</sup> Who smote the Egyptians in their first born, For to everlasting *endureth* his mercy.

<sup>11</sup> And brought out Israel from the midst of them, For to everlasting *endureth* his mercy.

<sup>12</sup> With a hand of strength and an arm stretched out, For to everlasting *endureth* his mercy.

<sup>13</sup> Who divided the Red Sea into divisions, For to everlasting *endureth* his mercy.

<!-- PAGE 1027 -->

<sup>14</sup> And made Israel to pass through the midst of it, For to everlasting *endureth* his mercy.

<sup>15</sup> And cast Pharaoh and his host headlong into the Red Sea, For to everlasting *endureth* his mercy.

<sup>16</sup> And led his people through the wilderness, For to everlasting *endureth* his mercy.

<sup>17</sup> Who smote great kings, For to everlasting *endureth* his mercy.

<sup>18</sup> And slew renowned kings, For to everlasting *endureth* his mercy.

<sup>19</sup> Sihon, king of the Arnorites, For to everlasting *endureth* his mercy.

<sup>20</sup> And Og, the king of Bashan, For to everlasting *endureth* his mercy.

<sup>21</sup> And gave their land for an heritage, For to everlasting *endureth* his mercy.

<sup>22</sup> An heritage to Israel his servant, For to everlasting *endureth* his mercy.

<sup>23</sup> Who in our humiliation remembered us, For to everlasting *endureth* his mercy.

<sup>24</sup> And rescued us from our oppressors, For to everlasting *endureth* his mercy.

<sup>25</sup> Who giveth food to all flesh, For to everlasting *endureth* his mercy.

<sup>26</sup> Make acknowledgments to the God of heavens, For to everlasting *endureth* his mercy.

<p class="title-block-h2" style="text-align:center; font-size:16px; font-weight:bold; margin:18px 0 12px;" markdown="1"><span style="color:#006411">PSALM 137</span></p>

<sup>1</sup> By the rivers of Babylon, there we sat down, Yea we wept when we remembered thee, O Zion!

<!-- PAGE 1028 -->

<sup>2</sup> Upon the willows, in the midst thereof, We hanged our harps.

<sup>3</sup> Then they required of us, they who carried us away captive, the words of a song, And, when we were in suspense, mirth, [*Saying*] Sing us one of the songs of Zion.”

<sup>4</sup> How shall we sing the song of Jehovah, In a strange land?

<sup>5</sup> If I shall forget thee, O Jerusalem! Forget let my right hand [*its cunning*.] <sup>6</sup> Cleave let my tongue to my palate, If I do not remember thee, If I do not raise Jerusalem above my highest joy. <span style="color:#800000">ff152</span> <sup>7</sup> Remember, O Jehovah! the children of Edom in the day of Jerusalem: ‘Who said, “Lay it bare — “Lay it bare, even to the foundations thereof.”

<sup>8</sup> O daughter of Babylon laid waste! Happy he who shall repay thee The retribution with which thou hast retributed us!

<sup>9</sup> Happy he who shall take And dash thy little ones against the stones.

<p class="title-block-h2" style="text-align:center; font-size:16px; font-weight:bold; margin:18px 0 12px;" markdown="1"><span style="color:#006411">PSALM 138</span></p>

<p style="text-align:center; color:#000080; margin:14px 2em;"><span style="color:#000080">*Of David*</span></p>

<sup>1</sup> I will celebrate thee with my whole heart; Before the gods! will sing psalms to thee.

<sup>2</sup> I will worship *thee* towards the temple of thy holiness, And sing to thy name for thy mercy and for thy truth; For thou hast magnified above all *things* thy name by thy word.

<sup>3</sup> In the day *when*, I cried *to thee*, then thou heardest me, Thou didst abundantly minister to me strength in my soul.

<sup>4</sup> Celebrate thee, O Jehovah! let all the kings of the earth; For they have heard the words of thy mouth.

<!-- PAGE 1029 -->

<p style="text-align:center" markdown="1"><sup>5</sup> And let them sing in the ways of Jehovah, For great is the glory of Jehovah.</p>

<sup>6</sup> For high *is* Jehovah; yet the lowly he will regard; And being high will know afar off:

<sup>7</sup> If I shall walk in the midst of trouble, thou wilt revive me; Against the wrath of my enemies thou wilt put forth thy hand, And save me shall thy right hand.

<sup>8</sup> Jehovah will perform in me *his work*, O Jehovah! thy mercy is from everlasting; The works of thy hands thou wilt not forsake.

<p class="title-block-h2" style="text-align:center; font-size:16px; font-weight:bold; margin:18px 0 12px;" markdown="1"><span style="color:#006411">PSALM 139</span></p>

<p style="text-align:center; color:#000080; margin:14px 2em;"><span style="color:#000080">*To the Chief Musician. A Psalm of David*</span></p>

<sup>1</sup> O Jehovah! thou hast searched me and known *me*.

<sup>2</sup> Thou hast known my down sitting and my uprising, Thou understandest my thought afar off.

<sup>3</sup> My path and my lying down thou besiegest, And with all my ways thou art acquainted.

<sup>4</sup> For there is not a word in my tongue [*But*] lo! O God! thou knowest it altogether.

<p style="text-align:center" markdown="1"><sup>5</sup> Behind and before thou hast shut me up, And hast laid upon me thy hand.</p>

<sup>6</sup> Wonderful is *thy* knowledge above me; It is high, I cannot *attain* to it.

<sup>7</sup> Whither shall I go from thy Spirit? And whither from thy time shall I flee?

<p style="text-align:center" markdown="1"><sup>8</sup> If I ascend up into the heavens, there thou art; If I lie down  <span style="color:#800000">ff153</span></p>

<p style="margin-left:2em;" markdown="1">in the sepulcher, behold! thou [*art there*.]</p>

<!-- PAGE 1030 -->
<sup>9</sup> Should I take the wings of the morning, That I may dwell in the uttermost parts of the sea, <sup>10</sup> Even there thy hand shall lead me, And hold me shall thy right hand.

<sup>11</sup> If I should say: “At least the darkness shall cover me; “And the night shall be light for me; “ <sup>12</sup> Even the darkness shall not hide from the And the night as the day shall shine, And the darkness as the light.

<sup>13</sup> For thou hast possessed my reins; Thou hast covered me in the womb of my mother.

<sup>14</sup> I will celebrate thee; for I have been fearfully and wonderfully made:  <span style="color:#800000">ff154</span> Marvelous are thy works, And my soul shall know them full well.

<sup>15</sup> Not hidden is my strength from thee, Which thou hast made in secret: I was woven together in the lowest parts of the earth.

<sup>16</sup> My shapelessness see did thine eyes; <span style="color:#800000">ff155</span> In thy book all shall be written; They were formed *in many* days, And *there was* not one of them.

<sup>17</sup> And towards me how precious are thy thoughts, O God! How great *are* the sums of them!

<sup>18</sup> If I should count them; above the sand they shall be multiplied: I am awakened, and still I am with thee.

<sup>19</sup> If thou shalt slay, O God! the wicked, Then ye men of bloods depart from me.

<sup>20</sup> Who have spoken of thee wickedly: Taken thy name falsely have thy adversaries.

<sup>21</sup> Thy haters, O Jehovah! shall not I hate? And with those that rise up against thee shall I not strive?

<sup>22</sup> With perfect hatred I have hated them, As my enemies I have held them.

<sup>23</sup> Search me, O God! and know my heart: Examine me and know my thoughts.

<!-- PAGE 1031 -->
